Sau khi thu đồ: \n1.Sư đồ cùng online, đệ tử nhận thêm 10% exp thêm.\n2.Giữa sư đồ dịch chuyển miễn phí.\n3.Sư đồ có thể tham gia sự kiện sư đồ hoặc phó bản sư đồ.\n4Mối người chơi đồng thời có thể nhận 5 đệ tử.\nChú ý mỗi nhân vật chỉ đồng thời bảo lưu 1 thân phận sư phụ hoặc đệ tử. Khi mi thành sư phục của người khác sẽ không thể bái sư.	Players Lv 20 or higher can take lower-level players as apprentices. When another player sends an apprenticeship request and you accept, a master-apprentice bond is formed. After taking an apprentice:\n1. When both are online, the apprentice gains +10% bonus EXP.\n2. Free teleportation between master and apprentice.\n3. Master and apprentice can join special mentorship events or dungeons.\n4. Each player can take up to 5 apprentices at the same time.\nNote: Each character can only be either a master or an apprentice. Once you become a master, you cannot apprentice under someone else.

Players Lv 40 or below can apprentice under a higher-level player. You can request apprenticeship via /c00ff00Nearby/p or the /c00ff00Mentorship List/p. After becoming an apprentice:\n1. When both are online, the apprentice gains +10% bonus EXP.\n2. Free teleportation between master and apprentice.\n3. Master and apprentice can participate in mentorship events and dungeons.\n4. Once you have been an apprentice for 3 days, whenever you top up, your master receives a Gold Leaf reward equal to 5% of your recharge.
